```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   118→    plan: PlanModel,
   119→    state: StateModel,
   120→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   121→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   122→
   123→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   124→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   125→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   126→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   127→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   128→    """
   129→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   130→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   131→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   132→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   133→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   134→
   135→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   136→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   137→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   138→        fid for fid in order
   139→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   140→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   141→        and fid not in stale_ids
   142→    ]
   143→    for fid in to_remove:
   144→        order.remove(fid)
   145→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   146→
   147→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   148→    existing = set(order)
   149→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   150→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   151→        if uid not in existing:
   152→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   153→            result.injected.append(uid)
   154→
   155→    return result
   156→
   157→
   158→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159→# Stale dimension sync helpers
   160→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→
   162→def _has_objective_backlog(
   163→    state: StateModel,
   164→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   165→) -> bool:
   166→    if policy is not None:
   167→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   168→    return any(
   169→        issue.get("status") == "open"
   170→        and issue.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   171→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   172→        for issue in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   173→    )
   174→
   175→
   176→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   177→    order: list[str],
   178→    *,
   179→    keep_ids: set[str],
   180→    pruned: list[str],
   181→) -> None:
   182→    to_remove = [
   183→        fid for fid in order
   184→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in keep_ids
   185→    ]
   186→    for fid in to_remove:
   187→        order.remove(fid)
   188→        pruned.append(fid)
   189→
   190→
   191→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   192→    order: list[str],
   193→    plan: PlanModel,
   194→    *,
   195→    inject_ids: set[str],
   196→    to_front: bool,
   197→    injected: list[str],
   198→) -> None:
   199→    existing = set(order)
   200→    if to_front:
   201→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   202→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   203→            if sid not in existing:
   204→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   205→                injected.append(sid)
   206→                existing.add(sid)
   207→        return
   208→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   209→        if sid not in existing:
   210→            order.append(sid)
   211→            injected.append(sid)
   212→            existing.add(sid)
   213→
   214→
   215→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   217→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   218→
   219→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   220→    plan: PlanModel,
   221→    state: StateModel,
   222→    *,
   223→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   224→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   225→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   226→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   227→
   228→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   229→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   230→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   231→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   232→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   233→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   234→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   235→    """
   236→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   237→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   238→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   239→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   240→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   241→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   242→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   243→
   244→    _prune_subjective_ids(
   245→        order,
   246→        keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids,
   247→        pruned=result.pruned,
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   251→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   252→
   253→    if not should_inject:
   254→        _prune_subjective_ids(
   255→            order,
   256→            keep_ids=(set(order) - injectable_ids),
   257→            pruned=result.pruned,
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   261→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   262→            order,
   263→            plan,
   264→            inject_ids=injectable_ids,
   265→            to_front=bool(cycle_just_completed and has_real_items),
   266→            injected=result.injected,
   267→        )
   268→
   269→    return result
   270→
   271→
   272→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   273→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   274→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   275→
   276→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   277→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   278→
   279→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   280→    """
   281→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   282→
   283→
   284→@dataclass
   285→class TriageSyncResult:
   286→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   287→
   288→    injected: bool = False
   289→    pruned: bool = False
   290→
   291→    @property
   292→    def changes(self) -> int:
   293→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   294→
   295→
   296→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   297→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   298→    return {
   299→        fid for fid, issue in issues.items()
   300→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   301→        and issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   302→    }
   303→
   304→
   305→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(state: StateModel, meta: dict) -> set[str]:
   306→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   307→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   308→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   309→
   310→
   311→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   312→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   313→        while sid in order:
   314→            order.remove(sid)
   315→
   316→
   317→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   318→    order: list[str],
   319→    plan: PlanModel,
   320→    confirmed: set[str],
   321→) -> bool:
   322→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   323→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   324→    existing = set(order)
   325→    injected_count = 0
   326→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   327→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   328→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   329→            injected_count += 1
   330→    return injected_count > 0
   331→
   332→
   333→def sync_triage_needed(
   334→    plan: PlanModel,
   335→    state: StateModel,
   336→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   337→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   338→
   339→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   340→
   341→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   342→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   343→
   344→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   345→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   346→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   347→    """
   348→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   349→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   350→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   351→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   352→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   353→
   354→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   355→
   356→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   357→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   358→
   359→    if already_present:
   360→        if last_hash and not confirmed and not _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta):
   361→            _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   362→            if current_hash:
   363→                meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   364→                plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   365→            result.pruned = True
   366→        return result
   367→
   368→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   369→        if _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta):
   370→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   371→        else:
   372→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   373→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   374→
   375→    return result
   376→
   377→
   378→@dataclass
   379→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   380→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   381→
   382→    injected: bool = False
   383→
   384→    @property
   385→    def changes(self) -> int:
   386→        return int(self.injected)
   387→
   388→
   389→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   390→    plan: PlanModel,
   391→    state: StateModel,
   392→    *,
   393→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   394→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   395→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   396→
   397→    Injects when:
   398→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   399→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   400→
   401→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   402→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   403→    """
   404→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   405→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   406→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   407→
   408→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   409→        return result
   410→
   411→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   412→    if policy is not None:
   413→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   414→            return result
   415→    else:
   416→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   417→        if unscored:
   418→            return result
   419→
   420→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   421→    insert_at = 0
   422→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   423→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   424→            insert_at = i + 1
   425→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   426→    result.injected = True
   427→    return result
   428→
   429→
   430→@dataclass
   431→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   432→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   433→
   434→    injected: bool = False
   435→
   436→    @property
   437→    def changes(self) -> int:
   438→        return int(self.injected)
   439→
   440→
   441→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   442→    plan: PlanModel,
   443→    state: StateModel,
   444→    *,
   445→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   446→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   447→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   448→
   449→    Only injects when:
   450→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   451→    - At least one objective issue exists
   452→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   453→    - No triage stages are pending
   454→    """
   455→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   456→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   457→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   458→
   459→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   460→        return result
   461→
   462→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   463→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   464→        return result
   465→
   466→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   467→    if policy is not None:
   468→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   469→            return result
   470→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   471→    else:
   472→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   473→        if unscored:
   474→            return result
   475→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   476→        has_objective = any(
   477→            f.get("status") == "open"
   478→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   479→            for f in issues.values()
   480→        )
   481→    if not has_objective:
   482→        return result
   483→
   484→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   485→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   486→    insert_at = 0
   487→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   488→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   489→            insert_at = i + 1
   490→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   491→    result.injected = True
   492→    return result
   493→
   494→
   495→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   496→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   497→
   498→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   499→    """
   500→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   501→
   502→
   503→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   504→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   505→
   506→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   507→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   508→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   509→    resolved since then.
   510→
   511→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   512→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   513→    through the plan.
   514→    """
   515→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   516→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   517→
   518→
   519→@dataclass
   520→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   521→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   522→
   523→    injected: bool = False
   524→
   525→    @property
   526→    def changes(self) -> int:
   527→        return int(self.injected)
   528→
   529→
   530→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   531→    plan: PlanModel,
   532→    state: StateModel,
   533→    *,
   534→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   535→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   536→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   537→
   538→    Only injects when:
   539→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   540→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   541→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   542→
   543→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   544→    """
   545→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   546→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   547→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   548→
   549→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   550→        return result
   551→
   552→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   553→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   554→        return result
   555→
   556→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   557→    insert_at = 0
   558→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   559→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   560→            insert_at = i + 1
   561→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   562→    result.injected = True
   563→    return result
   564→
   565→
   566→@dataclass
   567→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   568→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   569→
   570→    injected: bool = False
   571→
   572→    @property
   573→    def changes(self) -> int:
   574→        return int(self.injected)
   575→
   576→
   577→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   578→    plan: PlanModel,
   579→    state: StateModel,
   580→    *,
   581→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   582→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   583→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   584→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   585→
   586→    Injects when either:
   587→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   588→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   589→
   590→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   591→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   592→    """
   593→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   594→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   595→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   596→
   597→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   598→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   599→        return result
   600→
   601→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   602→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   603→
   604→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   605→    if not should_inject:
   606→        if policy is not None:
   607→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   608→        else:
   609→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   610→
   611→    if not should_inject:
   612→        return result
   613→
   614→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   615→    insert_at = 0
   616→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   617→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   618→            insert_at = i + 1
   619→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   620→    result.injected = True
   621→    return result
   622→
   623→
   624→__all__ = [
   625→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   626→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   627→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   628→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   629→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   630→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   631→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   632→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   633→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   634→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   635→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   636→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",
   637→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   638→    "ImportScoresSyncResult",
   639→    "ScoreCheckpointSyncResult",
   640→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   641→    "TriageSyncResult",
   642→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   643→    "current_under_target_ids",
   644→    "current_unscored_ids",
   645→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   646→    "is_triage_stale",
   647→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   648→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   649→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   650→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   651→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   652→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   653→    "sync_triage_needed",
   654→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   655→]
   656→
```

```
    22→def _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name: str | None):
    23→    """Load language-specific test coverage helpers from ``lang/<name>/test_coverage.py``."""
    24→    return get_lang_hook(lang_name, "test_coverage") or object()
    25→
    26→
    27→def _infer_lang_name(test_files: set[str], production_files: set[str]) -> str | None:
    28→    """Infer language from known file extensions when explicit lang is unavailable."""
    29→    paths = list(test_files) + list(production_files)
    30→    ext_to_lang = {
    31→        ".py": "python",
    32→        ".pyi": "python",
    33→        ".ts": "typescript",
    34→        ".tsx": "typescript",
    35→        ".js": "typescript",
    36→        ".jsx": "typescript",
    37→        ".cs": "csharp",
    38→    }
    39→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    40→    for file_path in paths:
    41→        suffix = Path(file_path).suffix.lower()
    42→        lang_name = ext_to_lang.get(suffix)
    43→        if not lang_name:
    44→            continue
    45→        counts[lang_name] = counts.get(lang_name, 0) + 1
    46→    if counts:
    47→        return max(counts.items(), key=lambda item: item[1])[0]
    48→    return None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _build_prod_module_index(production_files: set[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    52→    prod_by_module: dict[str, str] = {}
    53→    root_str = str(get_project_root()) + os.sep
    54→    for prod_file in production_files:
    55→        rel_file = prod_file[len(root_str) :] if prod_file.startswith(root_str) else prod_file
    56→        module_name = rel_file.replace("/", ".").replace("\\", ".")
    57→        if "." in module_name:

    59→        prod_by_module[module_name] = prod_file
    60→        if module_name.endswith(".__init__"):
    61→            prod_by_module[module_name[: -len(".__init__")]] = prod_file
    62→        parts = module_name.split(".")
    63→        if parts:
    64→            prod_by_module[parts[-1]] = prod_file
    65→    return prod_by_module

   121→
   122→
   123→def import_based_mapping(
   124→    graph: dict,
   125→    test_files: set[str],
   126→    production_files: set[str],
   127→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   128→) -> set[str]:
   129→    """Map test files to production files via import edges."""
   130→    lang_name = lang_name or _infer_lang_name(test_files, production_files)
   131→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
   132→    tested: set[str] = set()

   134→
   135→    for test_file in test_files:
   136→        graph_mapped = _graph_tested_imports(graph, test_file, production_files)
   137→        tested |= graph_mapped
   138→        if not graph_mapped or lang_name == "typescript":
   139→            tested |= _parse_test_imports(test_file, production_files, prod_by_module, lang_name)
   140→
   141→    barrel_basenames = set(getattr(mod, "BARREL_BASENAMES", set()))
   142→    tested |= _expand_barrel_targets(
   143→        tested=tested,
   144→        barrel_basenames=barrel_basenames,
   145→        production_files=production_files,
   146→        lang_name=lang_name,
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    has_logic = getattr(mod, "has_testable_logic", None)
   150→    tested |= _expand_facade_targets(
   151→        tested=tested,
   152→        graph=graph,
   153→        production_files=production_files,
   154→        has_logic=has_logic,
   155→    )
   156→    return tested
   157→
   158→
   159→def _resolve_import(
   160→    spec: str,
   161→    test_path: str,
   162→    production_files: set[str],
   163→    lang_name: str | None,
   164→) -> str | None:
   165→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
   166→    resolver = getattr(mod, "resolve_import_spec", None)
   167→    if callable(resolver):
   168→        return resolver(spec, test_path, production_files)
   169→    return None
   170→
   171→
   172→def _resolve_barrel_reexports(
   173→    filepath: str,
   174→    production_files: set[str],
   175→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   176→) -> set[str]:
   177→    """Resolve one-hop re-exports using language-specific helpers."""
   178→    if lang_name is None:
   179→        lang_name = _infer_lang_name({filepath}, production_files)
   180→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
   181→    resolver = getattr(mod, "resolve_barrel_reexports", None)
   182→    if callable(resolver):
   183→        return resolver(filepath, production_files)
   184→    return set()
   185→
   186→
   187→def _parse_test_imports(
   188→    test_path: str,
   189→    production_files: set[str],
   190→    prod_by_module: dict[str, str],
   191→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   192→) -> set[str]:
   193→    """Parse import statements from a test file and resolve production files."""
   194→    tested = set()
   195→    read_result = read_coverage_file(test_path, context="coverage_import_mapping_parse")
   196→    if not read_result.ok:
   197→        return tested
   198→    content = read_result.content
   199→
   200→    if lang_name is None:
   201→        lang_name = _infer_lang_name({test_path}, production_files)
   202→
   203→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
   204→    parse_specs = getattr(mod, "parse_test_import_specs", None)
   205→    if not callable(parse_specs):
   206→        return tested
   207→
   208→    for spec in parse_specs(content):
   209→        if not spec:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        resolved = _resolve_import(spec, test_path, production_files, lang_name)
   213→        if resolved:
   214→            tested.add(resolved)
   215→            continue
   216→
   217→        # Fallback: module-name lookup with progressively shorter prefixes.
   218→        cleaned = spec.lstrip("./").replace("/", ".")
   219→        parts = cleaned.split(".")
   220→        for i in range(len(parts), 0, -1):
   221→            candidate = ".".join(parts[:i])
   222→            if candidate in prod_by_module:
   223→                tested.add(prod_by_module[candidate])
   224→                break
   225→
   226→    return tested
   227→
   228→
   229→def _map_test_to_source(
   230→    test_path: str,
   231→    production_set: set[str],
   232→    lang_name: str,
   233→) -> str | None:
   234→    """Match a test file to a production file using language conventions."""
   235→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
   236→    mapper = getattr(mod, "map_test_to_source", None)
   237→    if callable(mapper):
   238→        return mapper(test_path, production_set)
   239→    return None
   240→
   241→
   242→def naming_based_mapping(
   243→    test_files: set[str],
   244→    production_files: set[str],
   245→    lang_name: str,
   246→) -> set[str]:
   247→    """Map test files to production files by naming conventions."""
   248→    tested = set()
   249→
   250→    prod_by_basename: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   251→    for p in production_files:
   252→        bn = os.path.basename(p)
   253→        prod_by_basename.setdefault(bn, []).append(p)
   254→
   255→    for tf in test_files:
   256→        matched = _map_test_to_source(tf, production_files, lang_name)
   257→        if matched:
   258→            tested.add(matched)
   259→            continue
   260→
   261→        basename = os.path.basename(tf)
   262→        src_name = _strip_test_markers(basename, lang_name)
   263→        if src_name and src_name in prod_by_basename:
   264→            for p in prod_by_basename[src_name]:
   265→                tested.add(p)
   266→
   267→    return tested
   268→
   269→
   270→def _strip_test_markers(basename: str, lang_name: str) -> str | None:
   271→    """Strip test naming markers from a basename to derive source basename."""
   272→    mod = _load_lang_test_coverage_module(lang_name)
   273→    strip_markers = getattr(mod, "strip_test_markers", None)
   274→    if callable(strip_markers):
   275→        return strip_markers(basename)
   276→    return None
   277→
   278→
   279→def transitive_coverage(
   280→    directly_tested: set[str],
   281→    graph: dict,
   282→    production_files: set[str],
   283→) -> set[str]:
   284→    """BFS from directly-tested files through dep-graph imports."""
   285→    return transitive_coverage_core(directly_tested, graph, production_files)
   286→
   287→
   288→def analyze_test_quality(
   289→    test_files: set[str],
   290→    lang_name: str,
   291→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   292→    """Analyze test quality per file."""
   293→    return analyze_test_quality_core(
   294→        test_files,
   295→        lang_name,
   296→        load_lang_module=_load_lang_test_coverage_module,
   297→        read_coverage_file_fn=read_coverage_file,
   298→        logger=logger,
   299→    )
   300→
   301→
   302→def get_test_files_for_prod(
   303→    prod_file: str,
   304→    test_files: set[str],
   305→    graph: dict,
   306→    lang_name: str,
   307→    parsed_imports_by_test: dict[str, set[str]] | None = None,
   308→) -> list[str]:
   309→    """Find which test files exercise a given production file."""
   310→    return get_test_files_for_prod_core(
   311→        prod_file,
   312→        test_files,
   313→        graph,
   314→        lang_name,
   315→        parsed_imports_by_test,
   316→        parse_test_imports_fn=_parse_test_imports,
   317→        map_test_to_source_fn=_map_test_to_source,
   318→        project_root=str(get_project_root()),
   319→    )
   320→
   321→
   322→def build_test_import_index(
   323→    test_files: set[str],
   324→    production_files: set[str],
   325→    lang_name: str,
   326→) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
   327→    """Parse test import sources once, producing a test->production import index."""
   328→    return build_test_import_index_core(
   329→        test_files,
   330→        production_files,
   331→        lang_name,
   332→        parse_test_imports_fn=_parse_test_imports,
   333→        project_root=str(get_project_root()),
   334→    )
   335→
```

```
1→"""Internal sync helpers for auto-cluster regeneration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_strategy import (
     9→    cluster_name_from_key as _cluster_name_from_key,
    10→    generate_action as _generate_action,
    11→    generate_description as _generate_description,
    12→    grouping_key as _grouping_key,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    15→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    16→    _current_stale_ids,
    17→    current_under_target_ids,
    18→    current_unscored_ids,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    21→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    22→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    34→
    35→
    36→def _sync_auto_cluster(
    37→    plan: dict,
    38→    clusters: dict,
    39→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
    40→    *,
    41→    cluster_key: str,
    42→    cluster_name: str,
    43→    member_ids: list[str],
    44→    description: str,
    45→    action: str,
    46→    now: str,
    47→    optional: bool = False,
    48→) -> int:
    49→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
    50→    changes = 0
    51→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
    52→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
    53→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    54→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    55→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
    56→        if (
    57→            old_ids != new_ids_set
    58→            or cluster.get("description") != description
    59→            or cluster.get("action") != action
    60→        ):
    61→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
    62→            cluster["description"] = description
    63→            cluster["action"] = action
    64→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
    65→            changes = 1
    66→    else:
    67→        new_cluster = {
    68→            "name": cluster_name,
    69→            "description": description,
    70→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
    71→            "created_at": now,
    72→            "updated_at": now,
    73→            "auto": True,
    74→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
    75→            "action": action,
    76→            "user_modified": False,
    77→        }
    78→        if optional:
    79→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
    80→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
    81→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
    82→        changes = 1
    83→
    84→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    85→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
    86→    for fid in member_ids:
    87→        if fid not in overrides:
    88→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    89→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
    90→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
    91→
    92→    return changes
    93→
    94→
    95→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    96→    member_ids: set[str] = set()
    97→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    98→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    99→            member_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   100→    return member_ids
   101→
   102→
   103→def _group_clusterable_issues(
   104→    issues: dict,
   105→    *,
   106→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
   107→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
   108→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   109→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   110→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
   111→        if issue.get("status") != "open" or issue.get("suppressed") or fid in manual_member_ids:
   112→            continue
   113→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   114→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   115→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
   116→        if key is None:
   117→            continue
   118→        groups[key].append(fid)
   119→        issue_data[fid] = issue
   120→    filtered = {key: ids for key, ids in groups.items() if len(ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   121→    return filtered, issue_data
   122→
   123→
   124→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   125→    plan: dict,
   126→    *,
   127→    clusters: dict,
   128→    existing_name: str,
   129→    member_ids: list[str],
   130→    now: str,
   131→) -> int:
   132→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   133→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   134→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   135→    changes = 0
   136→    if new_ids:
   137→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   138→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
   139→        changes += 1
   140→
   141→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   142→    for fid in member_ids:
   143→        if fid not in overrides:
   144→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   145→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   146→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   147→    return changes
   148→
   149→
   150→def sync_issue_clusters(
   151→    plan: dict,
   152→    issues: dict,
   153→    clusters: dict,
   154→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   155→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   156→    now: str,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   159→    changes = 0
   160→    groups, issue_data = _group_clusterable_issues(
   161→        issues,
   162→        manual_member_ids=_manual_member_ids(clusters),
   163→    )
   164→
   165→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   166→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   167→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   168→
   169→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   170→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   171→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   172→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   173→
   174→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   175→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   176→
   177→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   178→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   179→
   180→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   181→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   182→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   183→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   184→                changes += _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   185→                    plan,
   186→                    clusters=clusters,
   187→                    existing_name=existing_name,
   188→                    member_ids=member_ids,
   189→                    now=now,
   190→                )
   191→                continue
   192→
   193→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   194→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   195→
   196→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   197→            plan,
   198→            clusters,
   199→            existing_by_key,
   200→            cluster_key=key,
   201→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   202→            member_ids=member_ids,
   203→            description=description,
   204→            action=action,
   205→            now=now,
   206→        )
   207→
   208→    return changes
   209→
   210→
   211→def _subjective_state_sets(
   212→    state: StateModel,
   213→    *,
   214→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   215→    target_strict: float,
   216→) -> tuple[set[str], set[str], set[str]]:
   217→    if policy is not None:
   218→        unscored = policy.unscored_ids
   219→        stale = policy.stale_ids
   220→        under_target = policy.under_target_ids
   221→    else:
   222→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   223→        stale = _current_stale_ids(state)
   224→        under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   225→    return unscored, stale, under_target
   226→
   227→
   228→def _has_objective_backlog(issues: dict, policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None) -> bool:
   229→    if policy is not None:
   230→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   231→    return any(
   232→        issue.get("status") == "open"
   233→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   234→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   235→        for issue in issues.values()
   236→    )
   237→
   238→
   239→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   240→    plan: dict,
   241→    state: StateModel,
   242→    issues: dict,
   243→    clusters: dict,
   244→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   245→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   246→    now: str,
   247→    *,
   248→    target_strict: float,
   249→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   250→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   251→) -> int:
   252→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   253→    changes = 0
   254→
   255→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   256→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   257→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   258→    )
   259→
   260→    unscored_state_ids, stale_state_ids, under_target_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   261→        state,
   262→        policy=policy,
   263→        target_strict=target_strict,
   264→    )
   265→
   266→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   267→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   268→    )
   269→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   270→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   271→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   272→    )
   273→
   274→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   275→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   276→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   277→        description = (
   278→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   279→        )
   280→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   281→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   282→            plan,
   283→            clusters,
   284→            existing_by_key,
   285→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   286→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   287→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   288→            description=description,
   289→            action=action,
   290→            now=now,
   291→        )
   292→
   293→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   294→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   295→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   296→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   297→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   298→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   299→            plan,
   300→            clusters,
   301→            existing_by_key,
   302→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   303→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   304→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   305→            description=description,
   306→            action=action,
   307→            now=now,
   308→        )
   309→
   310→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   311→
   312→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   313→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   314→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   315→    ut_prune = [
   316→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   317→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   318→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   319→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   320→        and fid in order
   321→    ]
   322→    for fid in ut_prune:
   323→        order.remove(fid)
   324→        changes += 1
   325→
   326→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   327→
   328→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   329→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   330→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   331→        description = (
   332→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   333→            f"dimensions under target score"
   334→        )
   335→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   336→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   337→            plan,
   338→            clusters,
   339→            existing_by_key,
   340→            [REDACTED],
   341→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   342→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   343→            description=description,
   344→            action=action,
   345→            now=now,
   346→            optional=True,
   347→        )
   348→
   349→        existing_order = set(order)
   350→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   351→            if fid not in existing_order:
   352→                order.append(fid)
   353→
   354→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   355→        objective_evict = [
   356→            fid for fid in order
   357→            if fid in under_target_ids
   358→        ]
   359→        for fid in objective_evict:
   360→            order.remove(fid)
   361→            changes += 1
   362→
   363→    return changes
   364→
   365→
   366→def prune_stale_clusters(
   367→    plan: dict,
   368→    issues: dict,
   369→    clusters: dict,
   370→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   371→    now: str,
   372→) -> int:
   373→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   374→    changes = 0
   375→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   376→        cluster = clusters[name]
   377→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   378→            continue
   379→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   380→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   381→            continue
   382→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   383→            alive = [
   384→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   385→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   386→            ]
   387→            if alive:
   388→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])):
   389→                    cluster["issue_ids"] = alive
   390→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   391→                    changes += 1
   392→                continue
   393→        del clusters[name]
   394→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   395→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   396→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   397→                override["cluster"] = None
   398→                override["updated_at"] = now
   399→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   400→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   401→        changes += 1
   402→    return changes
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   406→
```

Three private helpers were added between the existing "Unscored dimension sync" section and the new "Stale dimension sync" section header:

- `_has_objective_backlog(state, policy)` — encapsulates the `policy.has_objective_backlog` / iterate-issues-dict branch that previously lived inline in `sync_stale_dimensions`.
- `_prune_subjective_ids(order, *, keep_ids, pruned)` — encapsulates the list-comprehension + remove loop pattern for pruning `subjective::*` IDs, taking a `keep_ids` allowlist and appending removed IDs to `pruned`.
- `_inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, *, inject_ids, to_front, injected)` — encapsulates both the front-of-queue (via `promoted_insertion_index`) and back-of-queue append paths, controlled by `to_front`.

**Change 2 — `sync_stale_dimensions` body replaced (lines 236-269)**

The ~65-line inline body was replaced with ~30 lines that delegate to the new helpers:

- `_prune_all_triage_stages(order)` — removes all `TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS` entries from the order list.
- `_inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)` — inserts unconfirmed triage stage IDs at `promoted_insertion_index`, returns `bool` indicating whether any were injected.

**Change 4 — `sync_triage_needed` body replaced (lines 348-375)**

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **`git stash` destroyed all staged changes**: Running `git stash && pytest && git stash pop` to check if test failures were pre-existing caused all 18+ decomposition changes to be lost. The stash pop restored files as unstaged, and an external process reverted them to original form. **Fix**: Avoided stash entirely in the redo. Strategy: apply changes, stage immediately, test, commit.
   - **External process reverting edits**: prompt_template.py and plan_reconcile.py edits were reverted by "user or linter" within seconds. **Fix**: For prompt_template.py, discovered the file was completely restructured by external process — found the target text now lives in `prompt_sections.py`. For plan_reconcile.py, re-applied the edit after re-reading.
   - **display.py `plan` variable bug**: Initially passed `meta.get("plan")` instead of `plan` to `print_stage_progress`. **Fix**: Added `plan` parameter to `_print_dashboard_header` signature and fixed the call site.
   - **Test failure — source inspection**: `test_scoring_allowed_subjective_uses_full_scorecard` uses `inspect.getsource()` to verify `_update_objective_health` contains "load_dimensions_for_lang". After decomposition, that call moved to `_resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions`. **Fix**: NOT YET FIXED — this is the current pending issue.
   - **Previous session filtering.py bug**: `_matches_issue_name_segment` returned `None` instead of `False` because `_HEX8_RE.match()` returns `None` on no match, and `and` chain short-circuits to `None`. **Fix**: External process version uses guard clause `if len(segments) < 3 or not _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment): return False`.
